_If you were looking for the specific scarves the player and partner wear in [Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Super_Mystery_Dungeon "Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon")
, see [Harmony Scarf](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Harmony_Scarf "Harmony Scarf")
._

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| [![](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/thumb/7/73/0018Pidgeot.png/100px-0018Pidgeot.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:0018Pidgeot.png) | **It has been suggested that this article be moved to [Equipment (Mystery Dungeon)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Equipment_(Mystery_Dungeon))<br>.**  <br>Please discuss whether or not to move it on its [talk page](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Talk:Scarf_(Mystery_Dungeon))<br>. |

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| [![](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/thumb/a/a6/0050Diglett.png/100px-0050Diglett.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:0050Diglett.png) | **This article is incomplete.**  <br>Please feel free to edit this article to add missing information and complete it.  <br>Reason: Locations in games other than Rescue Team, prices; translation of Japanese names for new equpiment in RTDX |

* * *

In the [Pokémon Mystery Dungeon series](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon_series "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon series")
, there are hold-items mainly called **scarves**, **bands**, **bows**, or **ribbons**, that typically increase statistics or make the Pokémon immune to certain [status conditions](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Status_condition_(Mystery_Dungeon) "Status condition (Mystery Dungeon)")
 when held.

Contents

Availability
------------

Available items vary depending on the game. In [Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Super_Mystery_Dungeon "Pokémon Super Mystery Dungeon")
, these items do not exist and many of the effects instead exist on [looplets](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Looplet "Looplet")
.

| **[MDTD](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Explorers_of_Time_and_Explorers_of_Darkness "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time and Explorers of Darkness")<br>[S](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Explorers_of_Sky "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Sky")<br>** | A held item that prevents the Pokémon's items from getting sticky and gunked up by a trap. |
| **[MDGTI](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Gates_to_Infinity "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Gates to Infinity")<br>** | If the leader holds it, carried items won't become sticky as the result of a trap. |
| **[MDRTDX](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Rescue_Team_DX "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Rescue Team DX")<br>** | If the leader equips it, it prevents items in your Toolbox from getting sticky as a result of traps or other means. |

Aura Bows
---------

The player can have one of these Bows, depending on what aura the player got in the beginning of the game.

[![Blue Bow TDS](https://archives.bulbagarden.net/media/upload/6/62/MDBag_Blue_Bow_TDS_Sprite.png)](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/File:MDBag_Blue_Bow_TDS_Sprite.png "Blue Bow TDS")

In animation
------------

In _[Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Team Go-Getters Out of the Gate!](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Team_Go-Getters_Out_of_the_Gate! "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Team Go-Getters Out of the Gate!")
_, all of the members of [Team Go-Getters](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Team_Go-Getters "Team Go-Getters")
 received one Pecha Scarf each from Aunty [Kangaskhan](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Kangaskhan_(Pok%C3%A9mon) "Kangaskhan (Pokémon)")
. The Scarves later protected the trio from the [Poison Powder](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Poison_Powder_(move) "Poison Powder (move)")
 attack of a group of [Shroomish](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Shroomish_(Pok%C3%A9mon) "Shroomish (Pokémon)")
.

In _[Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time & Darkness](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Explorers_of_Time_%26_Darkness "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Time & Darkness")
_, [Sunflora](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Sunflora_(Explorers_of_Time,_Darkness,_and_Sky) "Sunflora (Explorers of Time, Darkness, and Sky)")
 bought equipment for each member of [Team Poképals](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Team_Pok%C3%A9pals "Team Poképals")
, including herself. [Piplup](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Piplup_(Pok%C3%A9mon) "Piplup (Pokémon)")
 got a Joy Ribbon, [Chimchar](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Chimchar_(Pok%C3%A9mon) "Chimchar (Pokémon)")
 got a Defense Scarf, and Sunflora got a Power Band. The two former ones reappeared in _[Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Sky - Beyond Time & Darkness](https://bulbapedia.bulbagarden.net/wiki/Pok%C3%A9mon_Mystery_Dungeon:_Explorers_of_Sky_-_Beyond_Time_%26_Darkness "Pokémon Mystery Dungeon: Explorers of Sky - Beyond Time & Darkness")
_.
